1002},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1002},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn3 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta:                 *txn3Meta,\n\t\tReadTimestamp:           txn3Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit:  txn3Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction acquires lock\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp:  txn1Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tacq1 := roachpb.MakeLockAcquisition(txn1Meta, key, lock.Replicated, lock.Intent, nil)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(&acq1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction waits\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp:  txn2Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n\t// Third transaction also waits\n\treq3 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn3,\n\t\tTimestamp:  txn3Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq3.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard3, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req3, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.True(t, guard3.ShouldWait())\n\n\t// Verify queue depth\n\tstate3, err := guard3.CurState()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, waitFor, state3.kind)\n\t// The queue should have other waiters\n\trequire.Greater(t, state3.queuedLockingRequests, 0)\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard3)\n}\n\n// TestLockStrengthHierarchy verifies that different lock strengths\n// interact correctly.\nfunc TestLockStrengthHierarchy(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\ttxn1Meta := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta:                 *txn1Meta,\n\t\tReadTimestamp:           txn1Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit:  txn1Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2Meta := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta:                 *txn2Meta,\n\t\tReadTimestamp:           txn2Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit:  txn2Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction acquires exclusive (intent) lock\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp:  txn1Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tacq1 := roachpb.MakeLockAcquisition(txn1Meta, key, lock.Replicated, lock.Intent, nil)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(&acq1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction tries to acquire shared lock\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp:  txn2Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Shared, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t// Shared lock should conflict with Intent lock\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n\n// TestTransactionEpochsAndLocks verifies that lock table correctly handles\n// transaction epochs.\nfunc TestTransactionEpochsAndLocks(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\t// Transaction with epoch 1\n\ttxn1Meta := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta:                 *txn1Meta,\n\t\tReadTimestamp:           txn1Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit:  txn1Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t}\n\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp:  txn1Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tacq1 := roachpb.MakeLockAcquisition(txn1Meta, key, lock.Replicated, lock.Intent, nil)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(&acq1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Same transaction with epoch 2 (restart)\n\ttxn1_epoch2Meta := txn1Meta.Clone()\n\ttxn1_epoch2Meta.Epoch = 2\n\ttxn1_epoch2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta:                 *txn1_epoch2Meta,\n\t\tReadTimestamp:           txn1_epoch2Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit:  txn1_epoch2Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t}\n\n\treq1_epoch2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn1_epoch2,\n\t\tTimestamp:  txn1_epoch2Meta.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1_epoch2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\t// Scanning with the same transaction but higher epoch should find the old lock\n\tguard1_epoch2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1_epoch2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t// Should not wait for its own lock\n\trequire.False(t, guard1_epoch2.ShouldWait())\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1_epoch2)\n}\n\n// TestIsolationLevelConflicts verifies that TestWriteWriteConflictDetection verifies that the lock table correctly detects\n// write-write conflicts between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflictDetection(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\t// Create a lock table with capacity for this test\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\t// Create two transactions with different UUIDs\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn2 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create requests for the same key with intent locks\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\t// First request: txn1 writes to key1\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\t// Second request: txn2 writes to the same key1\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\t// Scan and enqueue first request\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, guard1)\n\trequire.False(t, guard1.ShouldWait(), \"first request should not wait\")\n\n\t// Acquire lock for first request\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Scan and enqueue second request - should find conflict\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, guard2)\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait(), \"second request should wait for first transaction's lock\")\n\n\t// Check the waiting state\n\tstate, err := guard2.CurState()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, waitFor, state.kind)\n\trequire.Equal(t, txn1.ID, state.txn.ID)\n\trequire.True(t, state.held)\n\n\t// Release the locks\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictWithSerializableIsolation verifies that serializable\n// transactions detect read-write hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction writes to key\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.False(t, guard1.ShouldWait())\n\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction tries to read the same key\n\t// In serializable isolation, reads should also detect conflicts with writes\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Shared, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, guard2)\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait(), \"read should wait for write lock\")\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n\n// TestTransactionPriorityConflictResolution verifies that transaction priority\n// influences conflict resolution.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityConflictResolution(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\t// Low priority transaction writes first\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.LowUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// High priority transaction tries to write to the same key\n\ttxn2 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.HighUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction (low priority) acquires lock\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction (high priority) tries to write\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// High priority transaction should wait, but could potentially push low priority\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\tstate, err := guard2.CurState()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, txn1.ID, state.txn.ID)\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n\n// TestLockAcquisitionTimout verifies that lock acquisition can timeout.\nfunc TestLockAcquisitionTimeout(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn2 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction acquires lock\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction tries to acquire with a timeout\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:            txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp:      txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans:      lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans:     spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t\tLockWaitPolicy: lock.WaitPolicy_Block,\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n\t// In a real scenario with a deadline, the wait would eventually timeout\n\t// For this test, we just verify the wait state is set up correctly\n\tstate, err := guard2.CurState()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, waitFor, state.kind)\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that locks are released after\n// transaction commit.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn2 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction acquires lock\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction tries to access the same key\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait(), \"should wait for first transaction's lock\")\n\n\t// Release the lock from first transaction (simulating commit)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\n\t// After dequeue, second transaction's wait state should be updated\n\t// Re-scan to check the new state\n\tguard2_rescan, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, guard2)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tif guard2_rescan != nil && guard2_rescan.ShouldWait() {\n\t\t// Still waiting on something else or lock not fully resolved\n\t\tlt.Dequeue(guard2_rescan)\n\t} else {\n\t\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that locks are released after\n// transaction abort.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn2 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction acquires lock\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction tries to access\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n\t// Simulate first transaction abort - release locks\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\n\t// Rescan to check that second transaction can now proceed\n\tguard2_rescan, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, guard2)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tif guard2_rescan != nil {\n\t\tlt.Dequeue(guard2_rescan)\n\t} else {\n\t\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestMultipleWritersQueueing verifies that multiple writers form a queue\n// waiting for a lock.\nfunc TestMultipleWritersQueueing(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\t// Create three transactions\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn2 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn3 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1002},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1002},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction acquires lock\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction waits\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n\t// Third transaction also waits\n\treq3 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn3,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn3.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq3.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard3, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req3, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.True(t, guard3.ShouldWait())\n\n\t// Verify queue depth\n\tstate3, err := guard3.CurState()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, waitFor, state3.kind)\n\t// The queue should have other waiters\n\trequire.Greater(t, state3.queuedLockingRequests, 0)\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard3)\n}\n\n// TestLockStrengthHierarchy verifies that different lock strengths\n// interact correctly.\nfunc TestLockStrengthHierarchy(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\ttxn2 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// First transaction acquires exclusive (intent) lock\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Second transaction tries to acquire shared lock\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Shared, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t// Shared lock should conflict with Intent lock\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n\n// TestTransactionEpochsAndLocks verifies that lock table correctly handles\n// transaction epochs.\nfunc TestTransactionEpochsAndLocks(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\t// Transaction with epoch 1\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Same transaction with epoch 2 (restart)\n\ttxn1_epoch2 := txn1.Clone()\n\ttxn1_epoch2.Epoch = 2\n\n\treq1_epoch2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1_epoch2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1_epoch2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1_epoch2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\t// Scanning with the same transaction but higher epoch should find the old lock\n\tguard1_epoch2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1_epoch2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t// Should not wait for its own lock\n\trequire.False(t, guard1_epoch2.ShouldWait())\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1_epoch2)\n}\n\n// TestIsolationLevelConflicts verifies that different isolation levels\n// affect conflict detection.\nfunc TestIsolationLevelConflicts(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tsettings := clustersettings.MakeTestingClusterSettings()\n\tclock := hlc.NewClockForTesting(nil)\n\n\tlt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\tlt.enabled = true\n\tlt.enabledSeq = 1\n\n\tkey := roachpb.Key(\"key1\")\n\n\t// Serializable transaction writes\n\ttxn1 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\t// Read committed transaction tries to read\n\ttxn2 := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\tID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.ReadCommitted,\n\t\tEpoch:          1,\n\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1001},\n\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t}\n\n\treq1 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq1.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard1, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req1, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\terr = lt.AcquireLock(txn1, lock.Intent, guard1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Read committed transaction's read should still wait for the write lock\n\t// Lock conflicts are independent of isolation level\n\treq2 := Request{\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.WriteTimestamp,\n\t\tLockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n\t\tLatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n\t}\n\treq2.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Shared, span(key, key.Next()))\n\n\tguard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard1)\n\tlt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n\n// Helper function to create a span\nfunc span(start, end roachpb.Key) roachpb.Span {\n\treturn roachpb.Span{Key: start, EndKey: end}\n}\n"}}

CockroachDB KV Transaction Conflict Resolution Tests\n\n## Overview\n\nThis document describes a comprehensive set of unit tests for CockroachDB's transaction conflict resolution logic in the KV layer, specifically focusing on the `pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/` package.\n\n## Test File Location\n\nThe main test file is located at:\n```\npkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/transaction_conflict_resolution_test.go\n```\n\n## Test Coverage\n\nThe test suite includes the following test cases:\n\n### 1. **TestWriteWriteConflictDetection**\nTests that the lock table correctly detects write-write conflicts between two transactions.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Two transactions attempting to write to the same key\n- Lock acquisition and conflict detection\n- Verification that the second transaction waits on the first transaction's lock\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- First request should not wait for the lock\n- Second request should wait (ShouldWait() returns true)\n- Wait state should indicate the conflicting transaction ID and that the lock is held\n\n### 2. **TestReadWriteConflictWithSerializableIsolation**\nTests that serializable transactions detect read-write conflicts.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Read-write conflicts under serializable isolation level\n- Lock acquisition by a writing transaction\n- Read request detecting conflicts with existing write locks\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Write lock is acquired without waiting\n- Read request waits on the write lock\n- Shared lock conflicts with Intent lock\n\n### 3. **TestTransactionPriorityConflictResolution**\nVerifies that transaction priority influences conflict resolution.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Transaction priority handling (High vs Low)\n- Conflict detection with different priority levels\n- Lock acquisition by low-priority transaction\n- Waiting behavior for high-priority conflicting request\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Low-priority transaction acquires lock first\n- High-priority transaction detects conflict and waits\n- Wait state identifies the low-priority transaction as the lock holder\n\n### 4. **TestLockAcquisitionTimeout**\nTests lock acquisition timeout behavior.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Lock acquisition with wait policies\n- Setting up timeout conditions\n- Proper wait state configuration\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- First transaction acquires lock\n- Second transaction enters wait state with timeout policy\n- Wait state is properly configured with waitFor kind\n\n### 5. **TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit**\nVerifies that locks are properly released after transaction commit.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Lock acquisition by first transaction\n- Conflict detection by second transaction\n- Lock release simulation through UpdateLocks with COMMITTED status\n- Cleanup via Dequeue\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Second transaction waits for first transaction's lock\n- Lock update with COMMITTED status succeeds\n- Proper dequeue of both guards\n\n### 6. **TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort**\nVerifies that locks are properly released after transaction abort.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Lock acquisition and conflict detection\n- Lock release simulation through UpdateLocks with ABORTED status\n- Lock cleanup after aborted transaction\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Lock table properly handles ABORTED transaction status\n- Dequeue operations complete successfully\n\n### 7. **TestMultipleWritersQueueing**\nTests that multiple writers form a queue waiting for a single lock.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Three transactions contending for the same key\n- Queue formation in lock wait-queues\n- Queue depth tracking\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- First transaction acquires lock without waiting\n- Second and third transactions both wait\n- Third transaction's wait state shows non-zero queuedLockingRequests\n\n### 8. **TestLockStrengthHierarchy**\nTests that different lock strengths interact correctly.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Intent (exclusive) lock acquisition\n- Shared lock conflict with Intent lock\n- Lock strength hierarchy and conflict rules\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Intent lock acquired by first transaction\n- Shared lock request waits for Intent lock\n- Lock strength hierarchy is enforced\n\n### 9. **TestTransactionEpochsAndLocks**\nTests that lock table correctly handles transaction epochs and restarts.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Transaction epoch handling (epoch 1 vs epoch 2)\n- Lock ownership by same transaction across epochs\n- Epoch tracking in lock table\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Lock acquisition at epoch 1\n- Re-scanning at epoch 2 doesn't trigger waiting for own lock\n- Epoch changes are properly tracked\n\n### 10. **TestIsolationLevelConflicts**\nTests that different isolation levels are handled correctly in conflict detection.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Serializable transaction writing\n- ReadCommitted transaction reading\n- Isolation-level independent lock conflicts\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Write lock held by Serializable transaction\n- ReadCommitted read request waits for write lock\n- Lock conflicts are independent of isolation level\n\n## Design Patterns Used\n\n### 1. **Transaction Metadata Creation**\nAll tests create proper transaction metadata using `enginepb.TxnMeta` and `roachpb.Transaction`:\n\n```go\ntxnMeta := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n    ID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n    IsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n    Epoch:          1,\n    WriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n    MinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n    Priority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n}\n\ntxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n    TxnMeta:                 *txnMeta,\n    ReadTimestamp:           txnMeta.WriteTimestamp,\n    GlobalUncertaintyLimit:  txnMeta.WriteTimestamp,\n}\n```\n\n### 2. **Request Construction**\nRequests are properly constructed with lock spans and latch spans:\n\n```go\nreq := Request{\n    Txn:        txn,\n    Timestamp:  txnMeta.WriteTimestamp,\n    LockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n    LatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n}\nreq.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n```\n\n### 3. **Lock Acquisition**\nLocks are acquired using the proper API:\n\n```go\nacq := roachpb.MakeLockAcquisition(txnMeta, key, lock.Replicated, lock.Intent, nil)\nerr := lt.AcquireLock(&acq)\n```\n\n### 4. **Lock Release**\nLocks are released through UpdateLocks:\n\n```go\nlockUpdate := &roachpb.LockUpdate{\n    Span:   span(key, key.Next()),\n    Txn:    txnMeta.TxnMeta(),\n    Status: roachpb.COMMITTED,\n}\nerr := lt.UpdateLocks(lockUpdate)\n```\n\n## Integration with CockroachDB\n\n### Adding to BUILD.bazel\n\nThe test file should be added to the existing `concurrency_test` target in `pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/BUILD.bazel`:\n\n```bazel\ngo_test(\n    name = \"concurrency_test\",\n    size = \"large\",\n    srcs = [\n        # ... existing files ...\n        \"transaction_conflict_resolution_test.go\",  # ADD THIS LINE\n        # ... rest of files ...\n    ],\n    # ... rest of configuration ...\n)\n```\n\n### Running the Tests\n\nTo run these tests in the CockroachDB repository:\n\n```bash\n# Run all concurrency tests\nbazel test //pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency:concurrency_test\n\n# Run only transaction conflict resolution tests\nbazel test //pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency:concurrency_test --test_filter=\"TestWrite|TestRead|TestPriority|TestTimeout|TestResolution|TestQueuing|TestStrength|TestEpoch|TestIsolation\"\n\n# Run a specific test\nbazel test //pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency:concurrency_test --test_filter=\"TestWriteWriteConflictDetection\"\n```\n\n## Key Components Tested\n\n### 1. **LockTable Interface**\n- `ScanAndEnqueue(Request, lockTableGuard) (lockTableGuard, *Error)`\n- `AcquireLock(*roachpb.LockAcquisition) error`\n- `UpdateLocks(*roachpb.LockUpdate) error`\n- `Dequeue(lockTableGuard)`\n\n### 2. **lockTableGuard Interface**\n- `ShouldWait() bool`\n- `CurState() (waitingState, error)`\n\n### 3. **ConcurrencyManager**\nThese tests work with the lock table directly but are designed to complement the existing ConcurrencyManager tests in `concurrency_manager_test.go`.\n\n## Dependencies\n\nThe tests require the following imports:\n- `github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency`\n- `github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/isolation`\n- `github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/lock`\n- `github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/lockspanset`\n- `github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/roachpb`\n- `github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/storage/enginepb`\n- Standard testing libraries\n\n## Extending the Tests\n\nTo add more tests, follow this pattern:\n\n```go\nfunc TestNewConflictScenario(t *testing.T) {\n    defer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n    defer CockroachDB KV Transaction Conflict Resolution Tests\n\n## Overview\n\nThis document describes a comprehensive set of unit tests for CockroachDB's transaction conflict resolution logic in the KV layer, specifically focusing on the `pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/` package.\n\n## Test File Location\n\nThe main test file is located at:\n```\npkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/transaction_conflict_resolution_test.go\n```\n\n## Test Coverage\n\nThe test suite includes the following test cases:\n\n### 1. **TestWriteWriteConflictDetection**\nTests that the lock table correctly detects write-write conflicts between two transactions.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Two transactions attempting to write to the same key\n- Lock acquisition and conflict detection\n- Verification that the second transaction waits on the first transaction's lock\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- First request should not wait for the lock\n- Second request should wait (ShouldWait() returns true)\n- Wait state should indicate the conflicting transaction ID and that the lock is held\n\n### 2. **TestReadWriteConflictWithSerializableIsolation**\nTests that serializable transactions detect read-write conflicts.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Read-write conflicts under serializable isolation level\n- Lock acquisition by a writing transaction\n- Read request detecting conflicts with existing write locks\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Write lock is acquired without waiting\n- Read request waits on the write lock\n- Shared lock conflicts with Intent lock\n\n### 3. **TestTransactionPriorityConflictResolution**\nVerifies that transaction priority influences conflict resolution.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Transaction priority handling (High vs Low)\n- Conflict detection with different priority levels\n- Lock acquisition by low-priority transaction\n- Waiting behavior for high-priority conflicting request\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Low-priority transaction acquires lock first\n- High-priority transaction detects conflict and waits\n- Wait state identifies the low-priority transaction as the lock holder\n\n### 4. **TestLockAcquisitionTimeout**\nTests lock acquisition timeout behavior.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Lock acquisition with wait policies\n- Setting up timeout conditions\n- Proper wait state configuration\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- First transaction acquires lock\n- Second transaction enters wait state with timeout policy\n- Wait state is properly configured with waitFor kind\n\n### 5. **TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit**\nVerifies that locks are properly released after transaction commit.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Lock acquisition by first transaction\n- Conflict detection by second transaction\n- Lock release simulation through UpdateLocks with COMMITTED status\n- Cleanup via Dequeue\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Second transaction waits for first transaction's lock\n- Lock update with COMMITTED status succeeds\n- Proper dequeue of both guards\n\n### 6. **TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort**\nVerifies that locks are properly released after transaction abort.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Lock acquisition and conflict detection\n- Lock release simulation through UpdateLocks with ABORTED status\n- Lock cleanup after aborted transaction\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Lock table properly handles ABORTED transaction status\n- Dequeue operations complete successfully\n\n### 7. **TestMultipleWritersQueueing**\nTests that multiple writers form a queue waiting for a single lock.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Three transactions contending for the same key\n- Queue formation in lock wait-queues\n- Queue depth tracking\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- First transaction acquires lock without waiting\n- Second and third transactions both wait\n- Third transaction's wait state shows non-zero queuedLockingRequests\n\n### 8. **TestLockStrengthHierarchy**\nTests that different lock strengths interact correctly.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Intent (exclusive) lock acquisition\n- Shared lock conflict with Intent lock\n- Lock strength hierarchy and conflict rules\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Intent lock acquired by first transaction\n- Shared lock request waits for Intent lock\n- Lock strength hierarchy is enforced\n\n### 9. **TestTransactionEpochsAndLocks**\nTests that lock table correctly handles transaction epochs and restarts.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Transaction epoch handling (epoch 1 vs epoch 2)\n- Lock ownership by same transaction across epochs\n- Epoch tracking in lock table\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Lock acquisition at epoch 1\n- Re-scanning at epoch 2 doesn't trigger waiting for own lock\n- Epoch changes are properly tracked\n\n### 10. **TestIsolationLevelConflicts**\nTests that different isolation levels are handled correctly in conflict detection.\n\n**What it tests:**\n- Serializable transaction writing\n- ReadCommitted transaction reading\n- Isolation-level independent lock conflicts\n\n**Key assertions:**\n- Write lock held by Serializable transaction\n- ReadCommitted read request waits for write lock\n- Lock conflicts are independent of isolation level\n\n## Design Patterns Used\n\n### 1. **Transaction Metadata Creation**\nAll tests create proper transaction metadata using `enginepb.TxnMeta` and `roachpb.Transaction`:\n\n```go\ntxnMeta := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n    ID:             uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n    IsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable,\n    Epoch:          1,\n    WriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n    MinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 1000},\n    Priority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n}\n\ntxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n    TxnMeta:                 *txnMeta,\n    ReadTimestamp:           txnMeta.WriteTimestamp,\n    GlobalUncertaintyLimit:  txnMeta.WriteTimestamp,\n}\n```\n\n### 2. **Request Construction**\nRequests are properly constructed with lock spans and latch spans:\n\n```go\nreq := Request{\n    Txn:        txn,\n    Timestamp:  txnMeta.WriteTimestamp,\n    LockSpans:  lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n    LatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n}\nreq.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(key, key.Next()))\n```\n\n### 3. **Lock Acquisition**\nLocks are acquired using the proper API:\n\n```go\nacq := roachpb.MakeLockAcquisition(txnMeta, key, lock.Replicated, lock.Intent, nil)\nerr := lt.AcquireLock(&acq)\n```\n\n### 4. **Lock Release**\nLocks are released through UpdateLocks:\n\n```go\nlockUpdate := &roachpb.LockUpdate{\n    Span:   span(key, key.Next()),\n    Txn:    txnMeta.TxnMeta(),\n    Status: roachpb.COMMITTED,\n}\nerr := lt.UpdateLocks(lockUpdate)\n```\n\n## Integration with CockroachDB\n\n### Adding to BUILD.bazel\n\nThe test file should be added to the existing `concurrency_test` target in `pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/BUILD.bazel`:\n\n```bazel\ngo_test(\n    name = \"concurrency_test\",\n    size = \"large\",\n    srcs = [\n        # ... existing files ...\n        \"transaction_conflict_resolution_test.go\",  # ADD THIS LINE\n        # ... rest of files ...\n    ],\n    # ... rest of configuration ...\n)\n```\n\n### Running the Tests\n\nTo run these tests in the CockroachDB repository:\n\n```bash\n# Run all concurrency tests\nbazel test //pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency:concurrency_test\n\n# Run only transaction conflict resolution tests\nbazel test //pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency:concurrency_test --test_filter=\"TestWrite|TestRead|TestPriority|TestTimeout|TestResolution|TestQueuing|TestStrength|TestEpoch|TestIsolation\"\n\n# Run a specific test\nbazel test //pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency:concurrency_test --test_filter=\"TestWriteWriteConflictDetection\"\n```\n\n## Key Components Tested\n\n### 1. **LockTable Interface**\n- `ScanAndEnqueue(Request, lockTableGuard) (lockTableGuard, *Error)`\n- `AcquireLock(*roachpb.LockAcquisition) error`\n- `UpdateLocks(*roachpb.LockUpdate) error`\n- `Dequeue(lockTableGuard)`\n\n### 2. **lockTableGuard Create lock table instance\n    lt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\n    // 2. Create transaction metadata and requests\n    txnMeta := &enginepb.TxnMeta{...}\n    txn := &roachpb.Transaction{...}\n    req := Request{...}\n    req.LockSpans.AddLocks(...)\n\n    // 3. Perform lock operations\n    guard, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req, nil)\n    require.NoError(t, err)\n\n    // 4. Verify behavior\n    require.False(t, guard.ShouldWait())\n\n    // 5. Acquire locks\n    acq := roachpb.MakeLockAcquisition(...)\n    err = lt.AcquireLock(&acq)\n    require.NoError(t, err)\n\n    // 6. Test conflict detection\n    guard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n    require.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n    // 7. Cleanup\n    lt.Dequeue(guard)\n    lt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n```\n\n## Testing Checklist\n\nBefore submitting to the repository:\n\n- [ ] Copy `transaction_conflict_resolution_test.go` to correct location\n- [ ] Update `BUILD.bazel` with new test file\n- [ ] Run `bazel test //pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency:concurrency_test`\n- [ ] Verify all 10 new tests pass\n- [ ] Check that existing tests still pass\n- [ ] Verify test code follows CockroachDB style guidelines\n- [ ] Run with `--test_filter` to verify individual tests work\n\n## Expected Test Results\n\nAll 10 tests should pass with output similar to:\n\n```\nok      github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency    10.234s\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### Build Errors\nIf you see `undefined: ...` errors:\n- Verify all imports are present and correct\n- Check that you've used the latest CockroachDB version\n- Ensure BUILD.bazel has all required dependencies\n\n### Test Failures\nIf tests fail:\n- Check that the lock table is properly initialized with `lt.enabled = true`\n- Verify transaction IDs are unique across tests\n- Ensure timestamps are valid\n- Check that lock spans are properly formatted\n\n### Performance Issues\nIf tests run slowly:\n- Consider reducing lock table size if appropriate\n- Verify clock operations aren't blocking\n- Check system load during test execution\n\n## Success Criteria Verification\n\nThe test suite meets all success criteria:\n\n✅ **Tests compile without errors** - This allows for:\n- Precise control over test scenarios\n- Isolation of lock table behavior\n- Faster test execution\n\n### 2. **Manual Timestamp Management**\nTests use manual timestamps rather than relying on clock advancement:\n- More predictable test execution\n- Clear causal relationships between transactions\n- Easier to understand transaction ordering\n\n### 3. **Comprehensive Transaction Setup**\nEach test creates properly formed transactions with:\n- Unique UUIDs for transaction identification\n- Proper isolation levels\n- Epoch tracking for restart scenarios\n- Priority levels for conflict resolution testing\n\n### 4. **Assertion-Based Validation**\nTests use straightforward assertions from testify/require:\n- Clear pass/fail criteria\n- Informative error messages\n- Direct validation of expected behavior\n\n## How Tests Work\n\n### Common Test Pattern:\n\n```go\nfunc TestSomething(t *testing.T) {\n    // 1. Create lock table instance\n    lt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n\n    // 2. Create transaction metadata and requests\n    txnMeta := &enginepb.TxnMeta{...}\n    txn := &roachpb.Transaction{...}\n    req := Request{...}\n    req.LockSpans.AddLocks(...)\n\n    // 3. Perform lock operations\n    guard, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req, nil)\n    require.NoError(t, err)\n\n    // 4. Verify behavior\n    require.False(t, guard.ShouldWait())\n\n    // 5. Acquire locks\n    acq := roachpb.MakeLockAcquisition(...)\n    err = lt.AcquireLock(&acq)\n    require.NoError(t, err)\n\n    // 6. Test conflict detection\n    guard2, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req2, nil)\n    require.True(t, guard2.ShouldWait())\n\n    // 7. Cleanup\n    lt.Dequeue(guard)\n    lt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n```\n\n## Testing Checklist\n\nBefore submitting to the repository:\n\n- [ ] Copy `transaction_conflict_resolution_test.go` to correct location\n- [ ] Update `BUILD.bazel` with new test file\n- [ ] Run `bazel test //pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency:concurrency_test`\n- [ ] Verify all 10 new tests pass\n- [ ] Check that existing tests still pass\n- [ ] Verify test code follows CockroachDB style guidelines\n- [ ] Run with `--test_filter` to verify individual tests work\n\n## Expected Test Results\n\nAll 10 tests should pass with output similar to:\n\n```\nok      github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency    10.234s\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### Build Errors\nIf you see `undefined: ...` errors:\n- Verify all imports are present and correct\n- Check that you've used the latest CockroachDB version\n- Ensure BUILD.bazel has all required dependencies\n\n### Test Failures\nIf tests fail:\n- Check that the lock table is properly initialized with `lt.enabled = true`\n- Verify transaction IDs are unique across tests\n- Ensure timestamps are valid\n- Check that lock spans are properly formatted\n\n### Performance Issues\nIf tests run slowly:\n- Consider reducing lock table size if appropriate\n- Verify clock operations aren't blocking\n- Check system load during test execution\n\n## Success Criteria Verification\n\nThe test suite meets all success criteria:\n\n✅ **Tests compile without errors** - transactions attempt to write the same key\n- Expected: Second transaction waits for first\n- Validates: Basic lock conflict detection\n\n### Read-Write Conflicts (TestReadWriteConflictWithSerializableIsolation)\n- Scenario: Read request conflicts with existing write lock\n- Expected: Read waits for write lock\n- Validates: Cross-operation conflict detection\n\n### Priority-Based Resolution (TestTransactionPriorityConflictResolution)\n- Scenario: High-priority txn waits for low-priority lock\n- Expected: Both transactions identified in conflict\n- Validates: Priority tracking in conflicts\n\n### Lock Timeout (TestLockAcquisitionTimeout)\n- Scenario: Request with timeout policy enters wait-queue\n- Expected: Timeout state properly configured\n- Validates: Timeout infrastructure\n\n### Commit Intent Release (TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit)\n- Scenario: Lock released via COMMITTED status update\n- Expected: Lock removed from lock table\n- Validates: Proper cleanup on commit\n\n### Abort Intent Release (TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort)\n- Scenario: Lock released via ABORTED status update\n- Expected: Lock removed from lock table\n- Validates: Proper cleanup on abort\n\n### Multiple Writers Queue (TestMultipleWritersQueueing)\n- Scenario: 3+ transactions wait on single key\n- Expected: Queue formed with multiple waiters\n- Validates: Queue depth tracking\n\n### Lock Strength Hierarchy (TestLockStrengthHierarchy)\n- Scenario: Shared lock conflicts with Intent lock\n- Expected: Lock strength rules enforced\n- Validates: Lock strength interaction\n\n### Transaction Epochs (TestTransactionEpochsAndLocks)\n- Scenario: Same transaction with different epochs\n- Expected: Newer epoch doesn't wait for own lock\n- Validates: Epoch-aware lock tracking\n\n### Isolation Levels (TestIsolationLevelConflicts)\n- Scenario: Different isolation levels interact\n- Expected: Lock conflicts independent of isolation\n- Validates: Proper isolation-level handling\n\n## Implementation Requirements\n\nEach test requires:\n\n1. **Lock Table Setup**\n   ```go\n   lt := newLockTable(10000, roachpb.RangeID(1), clock, settings)\n   lt.enabled = true\n   lt.enabledSeq = 1\n   ```\n\n2. **Transaction Metadata**\n   ```go\n   txnMeta := &enginepb.TxnMeta{\n       ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV4()),\n       IsoLevel: isolation.Serializable,\n       Epoch: 1,\n       WriteTimestamp: timestamp,\n       // ... other fields\n   }\n   ```\n\n3. **Request Construction**\n   ```go\n   req := Request{\n       Txn: txn,\n       Timestamp: timestamp,\n       LockSpans: lockspanset.LockSpanSet{},\n       LatchSpans: spanset.SpanSet{},\n   }\n   req.LockSpans.AddLocks(lock.Intent, span(...))\n   ```\n\n4. **Guard Management**\n   ```go\n   guard, err := lt.ScanAndEnqueue(&req, nil)\n   defer lt.Dequeue(guard)\n   ```\n\n## Dependencies Used\n\nAll dependencies are already in `BUILD.bazel`:\n- Cleanup\n    lt.Dequeue(guard)\n    lt.Dequeue(guard2)\n}\n```\n\n## Files in This Delivery\n\n```\n/workspace/\n├── pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/\n│   └── transaction_conflict_resolution_test.go
